# Matched ideal rolling equilibrium and derivative component

The entry is `run_own_ideal_rolling_matched_equilibrium_v5(NEWoutDir)` in the existing ROOT MATLAB71796. It is source only and has not been executed or checked by MATLAB yet. ROOT must create a new purpose/output and preserve the returned status/error. Frozen166, V3, V4 and all physical failures remain unchanged.

This component addresses a specific consistency issue found after167: replacing fixed wheel material contacts with current-phase circular support changes the nominal constraint tangent and its unactuated static compatibility. It therefore reads the actual initial q and yaw from143 but **does not reuse143's old point-contact torque solution**. All30 q remain fixed. A bounded offline pitch/roll search puts own COM over the two ideal supports and levels them; a reference base height is then computed. This is an offline model reference, never a physical reset or state write.

The new hard static solve includes all36 generalized equations through independent QR rows, own gas2000 force,16 independent joint-only loop reactions,6 motor torques and two unilateral/friction contact forces. It retains the original40Nm/5.7759Nm peak bounds, nominal signed motor curves, PD/raw limits and crank/hip CAD target domain. No soft fallback can become a static PASS. It reports the original domain diagnostics, full residual vectors and actual nominal PD mapping separately. Source-level numerical alignment tolerances are offline solver diagnostics and add no runtime physical gate.

Only a matched hard solution with level ideal supports advances to full coupled6DOF M/B and finite force derivatives. Current-phase supports and constraint tangent are recomputed at each perturbation. The unactuated static residual, forward gravity stiffness column, closure/mimic residuals, rank and condition are saved. The old166 Q/R scales are metadata only; no new gain is solved or selected here. This makes the contact-model change reviewable before any controller or original-plant trial.

The ideal circle is derived from the own radial vertices and joint axis; it is explicitly a planning surrogate. The actual24-point rim has contact cusps demonstrated by the separately frozenV4 source CPU probe. This component does not edit that mesh, imitate PhysX contact, validate no-slip behavior or certify physical stability. ROOT can compare it with the already actual150 q-hold/static-FF/145 wheel-LQR baseline and separately decide the next original-plant purpose. MAIN165 reference learning proceeds independently. The backup's eventual genuine270mm jump remains unproven.

All source and later errors/results require ROOT ordinary Git archival; currently **Git pending**. No author MATLAB/SDK/remote/model/raw/GPU action occurred in this namespace.

function report=run_own_ideal_rolling_matched_equilibrium_v5(outDir)
% NEW ROOT-only matched equilibrium/model, not a changed collision plant.
assert(feature('getpid')==71796,'Use ROOT existing MATLAB71796.');
assert(~isfolder(outDir)&&~isfile(outDir),'Use unique NEW purpose/outDir.');
[ok,msg]=mkdir(outDir);assert(ok,msg);
T='C:/Users/lulu/Desktop/claude/mjcf/MOD4_H1_SCUT_STEP270_ON_210_E3_CONTINUATION_20261021';
B=fullfile(T,'MOD4_H1_FUDAN133_LINE_Z_RESOURCE_V134_20261030','payloads_v2','seed27');
matrixFile=fullfile(T,'H1_OWN_MOD4_TEACHER_DYNAMICS_GAS2000_SOURCE_V2_20261030','source','own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1.m');
contactFile=fullfile(T,'OWN_MOD4_LQR167_COLD_FAILURE_LINEARIZATION_REVIEW_SOURCE_V4_20261030','source','own_phase_invariant_rolling_contact_tangent_v4.m');
clipFile=fullfile(T,'H1_OWN_MOD4_TEACHER_ACTION6_FREE_INTEGRATOR_SOURCE_V1_20261030','source','dc_motor_signed_clip_from210_v1.m');
inputs=struct('key',{},'path',{},'bytes',{},'sha256',{},'read_count',{});
oldPath=path;restore=onCleanup(@()path(oldPath)); %#ok<NASGU>
try
 [P,m]=readJSON(fullfile(T,'H1_MATLAB_ACTUAL_INITIAL_BALANCE_EXECUTION_V143_20261030','ACTUAL_INITIAL_STATIC_SOURCE_V4A_ACTUAL.json'),'7457f690494bb5892ad1ef8c217b2d10dc97435123b5cc9383b09e3bc7f36375','143_actualinitial_q_and_yaw_not_old_equilibrium_reuse');inputs(end+1)=m;
 [C,m]=readJSON(fullfile(B,'job0','assets','import_contract.json'),'517cd6a4cc469455af08da75dc4b7f7cb596b375bafb4c3e566c7df5bc51df49','own_C');inputs(end+1)=m;
 [G,m]=readJSON(fullfile(B,'source','OWN_STEP_WHEEL_GEOMETRY_FROM_R2_SOURCE.json'),'461f6b44e6014c72a755a0db5136fa8d45bd5b53b6318ee4b38221a4c597bc4d','own_G');inputs(end+1)=m;
 [~,m]=readBytes(matrixFile,'0cd6eb40378130064a5e327c04508bf0682901ad5b51c8d298e72d7d0a238ef4','own_gas2000_M_h_J');inputs(end+1)=m;
 [~,m]=readBytes(contactFile,'8a64697bc92348bcca5c7f516541b8af82143dc97de80826cc3d5d4e725d994d','current_phase_ideal_circular_contact_not_PhysX');inputs(end+1)=m;
 [~,m]=readBytes(clipFile,'257a9753b8264d65634f01edf8ce47e4a27e3ee3a1f81bfcace42a090770015f','captured210_nominal_signedclip_not72_backend_claim');inputs(end+1)=m;
 addpath(fileparts(matrixFile),'-begin');addpath(fileparts(contactFile),'-begin');addpath(fileparts(clipFile),'-begin');
 assert(samePath(which('own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1'),matrixFile)&&samePath(which('own_phase_invariant_rolling_contact_tangent_v4'),contactFile)&&samePath(which('dc_motor_signed_clip_from210_v1'),clipFile));
 names=cellstr(string(P.named_joint_order));order={'left_crank','left_hip','right_crank','right_hip','left_wheel_spin','right_wheel_spin'};
 [found,act]=ismember(order,names);assert(all(found));q=P.actual_q30(:);qOriginal=q;assert(numel(q)==30&&all(isfinite(q)));
 yaw=P.reference_yaw_actual_initial_rad;angles0=[P.reference_pitch_rad;P.reference_roll_rad];
 % Fixed q30. Only an OFFLINE reference base rotation/height is solved.
 lower=angles0-[.15;.05];upper=angles0+[.15;.05];
 options=optimoptions('fmincon','Display','off','Algorithm','sqp','MaxIterations',100,'MaxFunctionEvaluations',1200, ...
  'FunctionTolerance',1e-16,'OptimalityTolerance',1e-10,'StepTolerance',1e-12);
 objective=@(a) alignment(C,G,names,q,yaw,a);
 [angles,value,angleFlag,angleSolver]=fmincon(objective,angles0,[],[],[],[],lower,upper,[],options);
 [~,alignmentError]=alignment(C,G,names,q,yaw,angles);
 s=stateAt(q,zeros(3,1),rz(yaw)*ry(angles(1))*rx(angles(2)));
 d0=own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1(C,G,names,s);points0=own_phase_invariant_rolling_contact_tangent_v4(C,G,names,d0);
 s.p=P.reference_root_position_OFFLINE_ONLY(:);s.p(3)=-mean(points0(3,:));
 d=own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1(C,G,names,s);[points,JC,contactReceipt]=own_phase_invariant_rolling_contact_tangent_v4(C,G,names,d);
 [~,rr,pivot]=qr(d.loop_J(:,7:36).','vector');diagR=abs(diag(rr));rankTolerance=1e-9*max([diagR;1]);rankLoop=sum(diagR>rankTolerance);loopRows=pivot(1:rankLoop);
 assert(rankLoop==16,'Own projected initial q needs sixteen joint-only loop rows.');
 context=struct('C',C,'G',G,'names',{names},'act',act,'guide',find(startsWith(names,'guide_roller_')),'loopRows',loopRows,'rowsA',[]);
 model=groundModel(context,s);context.rowsA=model.independent_constraint_rows;
 static=hardStatic(C,names,s,act,d,d.loop_J(loopRows,:),JC);
 report=struct('schema','OWN_MOD4_IDEAL_ROLLING_MATCHED_EQUILIBRIUM_SOURCE_V5', ...
  'status','MATCHED_IDEAL_ROLLING_STATIC_NOT_ESTABLISHED_NO_LINEARIZATION', ...
  'producer_pid',feature('getpid'),'inputs',inputs,'named_joint_order',{names},'actuation_order6',{order}, ...
  'fixed_actualinitial_q30',q,'q_change30',q-qOriginal,'qdomain_diagnostic',qDomain(C,names,q,act), ...
  'reference_pitch_roll_rad',angles,'old_authored_point_pitch_roll_rad',angles0, ...
  'offline_angle_search_box',[lower,upper],'angle_bounds_are_design_not_physical_limits',true, ...
  'angle_solver',struct('exitflag',angleFlag,'value',value,'solver',angleSolver,'COM_line_and_support_height_residual_m',alignmentError), ...
  'reference_p_OFFLINE_ONLY',s.p,'reference_R_world_from_chassis',s.R,'reference_COM_world',d.COM,'support_world',points, ...
  'contact_model',contactReceipt,'selected_loop_rows',loopRows,'joint_only_loop_rank',rankLoop, ...
  'all6_anchor_m',vecnorm(reshape(d.loop_phi(1:18),3,6)),'all4_mimic_residual',d.loop_phi(19:end), ...
  'nominal_gas',{d.gas},'static_solution',static,'model',model, ...
  'old143_tau0_used_for_new_static',false,'all_actual_plant_collisions_unchanged',true, ...
  'ideal_circle_is_offline_surrogate_not_mesh_edit',true,'physical_teacher_valid',false,'runtime_admission',false, ...
  'primary_SuperMimic_gate',false,'no_new_gain_or_weight_scan',true);
 aligned=angleFlag>0&&max(abs(alignmentError))<=1e-6&&max(abs(points(3,:)))<=1e-6;
 report.offline_flat_support_alignment_satisfied=aligned;
 report.alignment_tolerance_is_offline_solver_not_new_runtime_guard=true;
 if static.hard_equations_and_nominal_mapping_satisfied&&aligned
  tau=static.tau6_Nm;nominal=model.Tvirtual.'*(d.gas_generalized_force-d.h+d.B_action_torque_order6*tau);
  unactuated=null(model.Bvirtual.');
  epsPosition=[1e-4;1e-4;1e-5;1e-4;1e-5;1e-4];K=zeros(6);D=zeros(6);receipts=cell(12,1);
  for j=1:6
   sp=perturb(s,model.Tvirtual(:,j)*epsPosition(j));sm=perturb(s,-model.Tvirtual(:,j)*epsPosition(j));
   [fp,rp]=projectedForce(context,sp,tau);[fm,rm]=projectedForce(context,sm,tau);K(:,j)=(fp-fm)/(2*epsPosition(j));
   receipts{j}=struct('coordinate',j,'position_step',epsPosition(j),'plus',rp,'minus',rm);
   sp=s;sm=s;velocity=model.Tvirtual(:,j)*1e-4;sp.v=velocity(1:3);sp.w=velocity(4:6);sp.dq=velocity(7:36);sm.v=-sp.v;sm.w=-sp.w;sm.dq=-sp.dq;
   [fp,rp]=projectedForce(context,sp,tau);[fm,rm]=projectedForce(context,sm,tau);D(:,j)=(fp-fm)/(2e-4);
   receipts{6+j}=struct('coordinate',j,'velocity_step',1e-4,'plus',rp,'minus',rm);
  end
  report.status='RETURNED_MATCHED_IDEAL_ROLLING_STATIC_AND_DERIVATIVES_NOT_PHYSX_OR_CONTROLLER';
  report.nominal_static_tau6=tau;report.nominal_projected_force_residual6=nominal;
  report.unactuated_projected_static_residual=unactuated.'*(model.Tvirtual.'*(d.gas_generalized_force-d.h));
  report.coupled_force_position_derivative6=K;report.coupled_force_velocity_derivative6=D;report.finite_derivative_receipts=receipts;
  report.A_continuous12=[zeros(6),eye(6);model.Mvirtual\K,model.Mvirtual\D];
  report.B_continuous12_by6=[zeros(6);model.Mvirtual\model.Bvirtual];
  report.translation_stiffness_check=struct('ideal_flat_ground_expected','Approximately phase-invariant in x; small off-axis own COM effects and numerical residue must be reported', ...
   'Kforce_xx_N_m',K(1,1),'whole_forward_column',K(:,1),'no_automatic_physical_gate_or_zero_patch',true);
  % Keep old166 design weights literal as metadata. Do not solve/ship a new
  % gain before ROOT reviews this matched model against the original plant.
  report.unchanged166_weight_metadata=struct('position_scale',[.25,.12,.025,.15,.025,.15], ...
   'velocity_scale',[.6,1.5,.25,1.5,.25,1.5],'torque_scale',[15,15,15,15,3,3],'gain_computed',false);
 end
catch error
 report=struct('schema','OWN_MOD4_IDEAL_ROLLING_MATCHED_EQUILIBRIUM_SOURCE_V5','status','ORIGINAL_NEW_MATCHED_ROLLING_SOURCE_ERROR_RETAINED', ...
  'producer_pid',feature('getpid'),'inputs',inputs,'message',error.message,'identifier',error.identifier,'stack',error.stack, ...
  'full_report',getReport(error,'extended','hyperlinks','off'),'physical_teacher_valid',false,'primary_SuperMimic_gate',false);
end
writeNewJSON(fullfile(outDir,'OWN_MOD4_IDEAL_ROLLING_MATCHED_EQUILIBRIUM_V5_ACTUAL.json'),report);
end

function [value,errors]=alignment(C,G,names,q,yaw,angles)
s=stateAt(q,zeros(3,1),rz(yaw)*ry(angles(1))*rx(angles(2)));d=own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1(C,G,names,s);
points=own_phase_invariant_rolling_contact_tangent_v4(C,G,names,d);line=points(1:2,2)-points(1:2,1);offset=d.COM(1:2)-points(1:2,1);assert(norm(line)>.1);
distance=(line(2)*offset(1)-line(1)*offset(2))/norm(line);errors=[distance;points(3,1)-points(3,2)];value=sum((1000*errors).^2);
end
function s=stateAt(q,p,R),s=struct('q',q,'dq',zeros(30,1),'p',p,'R',R,'v',zeros(3,1),'w',zeros(3,1));end

function r=hardStatic(C,names,s,act,d,J,JC)
rankLoop=size(J,1);E=[d.B_action_torque_order6,J.',JC.'];b=d.h-d.gas_generalized_force;n=size(E,2);
peak=[C.leg_motor.peak_torque_nm*ones(4,1);C.wheel_motor.peak_torque_nm*ones(2,1)];
lb=[-peak;-inf(rankLoop+6,1)];ub=[peak;inf(rankLoop+6,1)];A=[];rhs=[];mu=.7;
for k=1:2
 ids=6+rankLoop+(k-1)*3+(1:3);lb(ids(3))=0;
 for sx=[-1,1],for sy=[-1,1],row=zeros(1,n);row(ids)=[sx,sy,-mu];A=[A;row];rhs=[rhs;0];end,end %#ok<AGROW>
end
for j=1:4,row=zeros(1,n);row(j)=1/60;A=[A;row;-row];rhs=[rhs;3-s.q(act(j));3+s.q(act(j))];end %#ok<AGROW>
for j=[1,3]
 row=zeros(1,n);row(j)=1/60;row(j+1)=-1/60;delta=s.q(act(j))-s.q(act(j+1));A=[A;row;-row];rhs=[rhs;.85-delta;delta+1.0843]; %#ok<AGROW>
end
for j=5:6,row=zeros(1,n);row(j)=1/.6;A=[A;row;-row];rhs=[rhs;60;60];end %#ok<AGROW>
[~,RR,pivot]=qr(E.','vector');diagonal=abs(diag(RR));tol=1e-10*max([diagonal;1]);rankE=sum(diagonal>tol);rows=pivot(1:rankE);
H=diag([1./peak.^2;1e-12*ones(rankLoop,1);1e-8/(d.total_mass_kg*9.81)^2*ones(6,1)]);
options=optimoptions('quadprog','Display','off','Algorithm','interior-point-convex','MaxIterations',400,'ConstraintTolerance',1e-9,'OptimalityTolerance',1e-9);
[z,value,flag,solver]=quadprog(H,zeros(n,1),A,rhs,E(rows,:),b(rows),lb,ub,[],options);
r=struct('status','HARD_STATIC_NOT_ESTABLISHED_NO_SOFT_FALLBACK','hard_equations_and_nominal_mapping_satisfied',false, ...
 'exitflag',flag,'objective',value,'solver',solver,'selected_equation_rows',rows,'rank',rankE,'rank_tolerance',tol, ...
 'unbounded_equation_consistency_residual36',b-E*pinv(E)*b,'mu_assumed_not_measured',mu,'used_soft_fallback',false);
if isempty(z)||any(~isfinite(z)),return;end
tau=z(1:6);lambda=z(7:6+rankLoop);forces=reshape(z(7+rankLoop:end),3,2);target=[s.q(act(1:4))+tau(1:4)/60;tau(5:6)/.6];raw=[target(1:4)/.5;target(5:6)/10];
processed=[.5*max(-6,min(6,raw(1:4)));10*max(-6,min(6,raw(5:6)))];
for j=[1,3],processed(j)=min(processed(j+1)+.85,max(processed(j+1)-1.0843,processed(j)));end
pd=[60*(processed(1:4)-s.q(act(1:4)));.6*processed(5:6)];
stall=[C.leg_motor.envelope_intercept_nm*ones(4,1);C.wheel_motor.envelope_intercept_nm*ones(2,1)];speed=[C.leg_motor.no_load_speed_rpm*ones(4,1);C.wheel_motor.no_load_speed_rpm*ones(2,1)]*pi/30;
first=dc_motor_signed_clip_from210_v1(pd,zeros(6,1),peak,stall,speed);actual=dc_motor_signed_clip_from210_v1(first,zeros(6,1),peak,stall,speed);
residual=E*z-b;mapped=d.B_action_torque_order6*actual+J.'*lambda+JC.'*forces(:)-b;violation=max([0;A*z-rhs;lb-z;z-ub]);
r.tau6_Nm=tau;r.loop_lambda_selected=lambda;r.ground_reaction_world_N=forces;r.full_residual36=residual;r.after_nominal_PD_clips_residual36=mapped;
r.root_force_residual_N=residual(1:3);r.root_moment_residual_Nm=residual(4:6);r.joint_generalized_residual30=residual(7:36);
r.max_mixed_unit_residual=max(abs(residual));r.inequality_violation=violation;r.raw6=raw;r.PD_target6=target;r.nominal_mapped_torque6=actual;r.peak_limits6_Nm=peak;
r.PD_targets_outside_q_domain_are_not_state_clamps=true;r.steady_queue_required_before_equivalent_torque=true;
r.hard_equations_and_nominal_mapping_satisfied=flag>0&&max(abs(residual))<=1e-6&&max(abs(mapped))<=1e-6&&violation<=1e-6;
if r.hard_equations_and_nominal_mapping_satisfied,r.status='MATCHED_IDEAL_ROLLING_HARD_STATIC_NOT_PHYSX_STABILITY';end
end

function model=groundModel(c,s)
d=own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1(c.C,c.G,c.names,s);[points,JC]=own_phase_invariant_rolling_contact_tangent_v4(c.C,c.G,c.names,d);v=virtualJ(c.C,c.G,d);
E=zeros(numel(c.guide),36);for j=1:numel(c.guide),E(j,6+c.guide(j))=1;end
Aall=[d.loop_J(c.loopRows,:);JC;E;v.yawRow];
if isempty(c.rowsA),[~,rr,pivot]=qr(Aall.','vector');diagonal=abs(diag(rr));tol=1e-8*max([diagonal;1]);rankA=sum(diagonal>tol);rows=pivot(1:rankA);else,rows=c.rowsA;rankA=numel(rows);tol=[];end
[~,~,V]=svd(Aall(rows,:));N=V(:,rankA+1:end);assert(size(N,2)==6,'Declared coupled rolling model has six DOFs.');
O=zeros(6,36);O(1,1:3)=v.headingX.';O(2,4:6)=v.headingY.';O(3:6,:)=v.Jworld;mapping=O*N;assert(rcond(mapping)>1e-12);
Tv=N/mapping;Bv=Tv.'*d.B_action_torque_order6;M=Tv.'*d.M*Tv;
model=struct('Tvirtual',Tv,'Mvirtual',M,'Bvirtual',Bv,'Mvirtual_eigenvalues',eig(M),'input_projection_rank',rank(Bv), ...
 'constraint_rank',rankA,'independent_constraint_rows',rows,'rank_tolerance',tol,'all_selected_constraint_tangent_max',max(abs(Aall(rows,:)*Tv),[],'all'), ...
 'all_constraint_tangent_max',max(abs(Aall*Tv),[],'all'),'all22_loop_tangent_max',max(abs(d.loop_J*Tv),[],'all'), ...
 'virtual',v,'contactWorld',points,'kinematic_mapping_condition',cond(mapping),'guide_rates_and_yaw_locked_planning_only',true);
end
function [f,r]=projectedForce(c,s,tau)
m=groundModel(c,s);d=own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1(c.C,c.G,c.names,s);f=m.Tvirtual.'*(d.gas_generalized_force-d.h+d.B_action_torque_order6*tau);
r=struct('all6_anchor_m',vecnorm(reshape(d.loop_phi(1:18),3,6)),'all4_mimic',d.loop_phi(19:end), ...
 'full_constraint_tangent_max',m.all_constraint_tangent_max,'qdomain_diagnostic',qDomain(c.C,c.names,s.q,c.act));
end
function v=virtualJ(C,G,d)
bn=cellstr(string(G.body_names));R=d.body_rotation(:,:,1);x=R(:,1);horizontal=hypot(x(1),x(2));assert(horizontal>1e-8);ex=[x(1);x(2);0]/horizontal;ey=[-ex(2);ex(1);0];ez=[0;0;1];
yawRow=zeros(1,36);yawRow(4:6)=cross(x,[-x(2);x(1);0]/horizontal^2).';Jworld=zeros(4,36);L=zeros(2,1);theta=L;
for j=1:2
 sides={'left','right'};[hp,Jh]=anchor(C.joints.([sides{j},'_hip']));[ap,Ja]=anchor(C.joints.([sides{j},'_wheel_spin']));r=hp-ap;Jr=Jh-Ja;rx=ex.'*r;rz=r(3);lateral=ey.'*r;
 L(j)=norm(r);theta(j)=atan2(rx,rz);Jworld(2*j-1,:)=r.'/L(j)*Jr;Jworld(2*j,:)=((rz*ex.'-rx*ez.')*Jr+rz*lateral*yawRow)/(rx^2+rz^2);
end
v=struct('length2',L,'theta_world2',theta,'body_phi',atan2(-R(3,1),horizontal),'headingX',ex,'headingY',ey,'yawRow',yawRow,'Jworld',Jworld);
 function [p,J]=anchor(row),b=find(strcmp(bn,row.parent),1);offset=d.body_rotation(:,:,b)*row.local_pos0(:);p=d.body_position(:,b)+offset;J=d.body_origin_Jv(:,:,b)-skew(offset)*d.body_Jw(:,:,b);end
end
function s=perturb(s,dx),s.p=s.p+dx(1:3);s.R=expm(skew(dx(4:6)))*s.R;s.q=s.q+dx(7:36);end
function r=qDomain(C,names,q,act)
bad=struct('name',{},'q',{},'limits',{});for j=1:30,lim=C.joints.(names{j}).limits_si;if ~isempty(lim)&&(q(j)<lim(1)||q(j)>lim(2)),bad(end+1)=struct('name',names{j},'q',q(j),'limits',lim);end,end %#ok<AGROW>
delta=q(act([1,3]))-q(act([2,4]));motor=q(act(1:4));sdkLower=[-1.2;-.5;-1.2;-.5];sdkUpper=[.9;.5;.9;.5];
r=struct('named_state_violations',bad,'motor_actual4',motor,'actual_SDK_motor_domain_lower4',sdkLower,'actual_SDK_motor_domain_upper4',sdkUpper, ...
 'actual_SDK_motor_domain_valid',all(motor>=sdkLower&motor<=sdkUpper),'crank_minus_hip2',delta,'CAD_in_domain',all(delta>=-1.0843&delta<=.85),'no_state_clamp_or_runtime_termination_added',true);
end
function [a,m]=readJSON(p,s,k),[b,m]=readBytes(p,s,k);a=jsondecode(native2unicode(b(:).','UTF-8'));end
function [b,m]=readBytes(p,s,k)
f=fopen(p,'rb');assert(f>=0);cl=onCleanup(@()fclose(f));b=fread(f,Inf,'*uint8');md=java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance('SHA-256');md.update(typecast(b(:),'int8'));got=lower(reshape(dec2hex(typecast(md.digest(),'uint8'),2).',1,[]));assert(strcmpi(got,s),['Pinned SHA ',k]);m=struct('key',k,'path',p,'bytes',numel(b),'sha256',got,'read_count',1);
end
function writeNewJSON(p,r),assert(~isfile(p));f=fopen(p,'wb');assert(f>=0);cl=onCleanup(@()fclose(f));b=[unicode2native(jsonencode(r,'PrettyPrint',true),'UTF-8'),uint8(10)];assert(fwrite(f,b,'uint8')==numel(b));end
function yes=samePath(a,b),yes=strcmpi(strrep(a,'\','/'),strrep(b,'\','/'));end
function S=skew(v),v=v(:);S=[0,-v(3),v(2);v(3),0,-v(1);-v(2),v(1),0];end
function R=rx(a),R=[1,0,0;0,cos(a),-sin(a);0,sin(a),cos(a)];end
function R=ry(a),R=[cos(a),0,sin(a);0,1,0;-sin(a),0,cos(a)];end
function R=rz(a),R=[cos(a),-sin(a),0;sin(a),cos(a),0;0,0,1];end

# Actual167 cold failure and contact-linearization review

ROOT's explicitly allowed derived167 report was read once after a frozen purpose and SHA receipt. The original19-control case ended at .19s with original bad_orientation only; pitch went .005307 to -.858441rad, root rose .130367m and final upward velocity was2.027878m/s.13 of18 measured decisions clipped requested effort before inverse PD. Hip requests reached219.555/218.537Nm; raw and CAD clip counts were zero. These are engineering requests, not measured motor torques. All original results and166/V3 sources remain unchanged.

The new gravity-only source CPU counterexample uses the actual own body masses and wheel radial vertices, not an external robot. Each wheel has24 outer radial points, radius .0637000011m and a constructed flat-face average depth .0631550387m. Placing the total own mass14.2201403kg on that isolated radial support gives fixed-material-centre stiffness+2208.845N/m at a .1mm derivative step, close in scale to166's full-body+2195.409N/m. A rotating fixed point is a pivot: axle height follows `h*cos(theta)`, and its gravity projection introduces positive longitudinal stiffness. A circular support recomputed from the current axle gives zero longitudinal gravity stiffness. An authored facet changes its contacting vertex: the same difference is negative and scales from -181408N/m at .1mm to -1834299N/m at .01mm. That cusp is not a smooth finite stiffness suitable for blind replacement in the original LQR derivative.

This establishes a source contact-approximation problem; it does not prove the sole cause of actual167. The166 derivative recomputes the constrained tangent at perturbed poses but retains the same lowest-face average material coordinates. It therefore differentiates the fixed-pivot geometry rather than the intended rolling wheel phase. Linear controllability, delay spectral radius and old small-error convergence were internally valid for that different planning model; they do not validate it against original PhysX.

The frozen166 input and V3 output units/order are consistent in the inspected sources: six motor torques in [left crank,left hip,right crank,right hip,left wheel,right wheel], metres/radians and rates for virtual state, original .5rad/10rad/s raw mapping, leg60/2 and wheel.6 PD, target-space CAD, original signed envelopes. The target queue remains implemented by original runtime. No source-only audit can establish actual applied effort, contact slip, synchronous runtime q order or delayed-target response from the aggregate167 report. The proposed first-four-decision export states the minimum fields and requires ROOT's separately frozen read purpose.

Applying the frozen gain to the exact last saved error reconstructs hip219.555/218.537 and wheel46.447/46.426Nm. The forward-position term alone contributes about+72.9Nm per crank,+41.3Nm per hip and+31.1Nm per wheel, while the left leg-length-rate term contributes+108.7Nm to left hip. Thus the false translation stiffness is an actionable modeling concern, but deleting one feedback column is not a justified causal repair. No weight scan or gain adjustment was performed.

`source/own_phase_invariant_rolling_contact_tangent_v4.m` supplies a minimal executable MATLAB contact-tangent comparison. It accepts own C/G/names and the existing own gas2000 tree result, computes ideal circular current-phase support from the own joint axis and authored radial radius, and returns points/Jcontact/assumption receipt. It does not solve a full controller or mutate physical geometry. Any full-body model comparison must recompute constraints, matched static equilibrium and force derivatives around that same contact model; old143 point-contact torques are not automatically an exact equilibrium after replacing contact geometry. Actual authored mesh/facet contact still belongs to original PhysX. ROOT owns MATLAB execution; the author has not run this component.

The next conservative physical baseline is the already actual150 combination: keep actual initial q with143 static feedforward and own145 wheel LQR, which completed2s under original guards. It is a known baseline, not a full4s/general stability or jumping guarantee. Do not redeploy the unmodified failed166/V3 gain or silently replace its false model by a torque clamp. A later coupled-model repair can use the phase-invariant tangent as an explicitly ideal rolling planning approximation and be tested separately against original plant.

def require(x,message):
 if not x:raise ValueError(message)
def sha(b):return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def qmat(a):
 w,x,y,z=np.asarray(a)/np.linalg.norm(a)
 return np.array([[1-2*(y*y+z*z),2*(x*y-w*z),2*(x*z+w*y)],
 [2*(x*y+w*z),1-2*(x*x+z*z),2*(y*z-w*x)],
 [2*(x*z-w*y),2*(y*z+w*x),1-2*(x*x+y*y)]])
def ry2(a):return np.array([[math.cos(a),math.sin(a)],[-math.sin(a),math.cos(a)]])

def run(R):
 start=time.monotonic();data={}
 for key,(path,expected) in FILES.items():
  b=path.read_bytes();s=sha(b);R['inputs'].append(dict(key=key,path=str(path),bytes=len(b),sha256=s,byte_reads=1,JSON_parses=1));require(s==expected,'Frozen source '+key);data[key]=json.loads(b)
 C,G,P=(data[k] for k in ('C','G','frozen_inline_parameters'));mass=sum(v['mass_kg'] for v in C['bodies'].values());mg=mass*9.81
 R['own_total_mass_kg']=mass;R['scope']='Gravity-only two-dimensional whole-mass-on-own-wheel counterexample; no own fullbody M/h/J solve, no LQR retune or actual trajectory replay.'
 wheels=[]
 for side in ('left','right'):
  name=side+'_wheel';shape=[s for s in G['shapes'] if s['body']==name];require(len(shape)==1,'One authored wheel shape')
  shape=shape[0];row=C['joints'][side+'_wheel_spin'];axis=qmat(row['local_rot1'])[:,0];axis/=np.linalg.norm(axis)
  candidate=np.array([1.,0.,0.]);candidate-=axis*np.dot(candidate,axis)
  if np.linalg.norm(candidate)<.1:candidate=np.array([0.,0.,1.]);candidate-=axis*np.dot(candidate,axis)
  e1=candidate/np.linalg.norm(candidate);e2=np.cross(e1,axis)
  points=np.asarray(shape['points'])@np.asarray(shape['linear_body_from_shape']).T+np.asarray(shape['origin_body_m'])
  relative=points-np.asarray(row['local_pos1']);radial=np.c_[relative@e1,relative@e2]
  radial=np.unique(np.round(radial,10),axis=0);radius=np.linalg.norm(radial,axis=1)
  # The source wheel includes paired side faces. Its outer rim defines the
  # support polygon in this isolated radial-plane contact calculation.
  rim=radial[radius>radius.max()-1e-7];angles=np.arctan2(rim[:,1],rim[:,0]);rim=rim[np.argsort(angles)];angles=np.sort(angles)
  require(len(rim)>=8,'Authored radial rim, not guessed foreign radius')
  gaps=np.mod(np.roll(angles,-1)-angles,2*math.pi);i=int(np.argmin(gaps));mid=angles[i]+gaps[i]/2
  # Reorient one authored support face horizontally. This is explicitly a
  # constructed contact phase, not the unknown actual167 wheel phase.
  radial0=rim@ry2(mid+math.pi/2).T
  bottom=radial0[np.abs(radial0[:,1]-radial0[:,1].min())<1e-8];require(len(bottom)>=2,'Constructed authored support face')
  material=bottom.mean(axis=0);require(abs(material[0])<1e-7,'Symmetric source support face');h=-float(material[1]);r=float(radius.max())
  def gravity_force(theta,mode):
   if mode=='frozen_material':cp=ry2(theta)@material
   elif mode=='updated_authored_mesh':
    v=radial0@ry2(theta).T;cp=v[np.abs(v[:,1]-v[:,1].min())<1e-10].mean(axis=0)
   elif mode=='updated_circle':cp=np.array([0.,-r])
   else:raise ValueError(mode)
   # No-slip instantaneous tangent: xdot=-cp_z*theta_dot,
   # zdot=cp_x*theta_dot. Project gravity onto xdot=1.
   return float(mg*cp[0]/cp[1]),cp
  derivatives=[]
  for eps in (1e-3,1e-4,1e-5):
   theta=eps/h;record=dict(forward_coordinate_step_m=eps,spin_step_rad=theta)
   for mode in ('frozen_material','updated_authored_mesh','updated_circle'):
    fp,cp=gravity_force(theta,mode);fm,cm=gravity_force(-theta,mode)
    record[mode]=dict(gravity_projected_force_plus_N=fp,gravity_projected_force_minus_N=fm,
     central_force_derivative_N_m=(fp-fm)/(2*eps),plus_contact_radial_m=cp.tolist(),minus_contact_radial_m=cm.tolist())
   require(record['frozen_material']['central_force_derivative_N_m']>0,'Frozen-pivot positive false stiffness')
   require(record['updated_authored_mesh']['central_force_derivative_N_m']<0,'Authored face phase changes contact side')
   require(abs(record['updated_circle']['central_force_derivative_N_m'])<1e-12,'Circle rolling translation invariance')
   derivatives.append(record)
  wheels.append(dict(side=side,radial_rim_points=len(rim),radius_from_own_shape_m=r,support_face_depth_m=h,
   fixed_point_mg_over_h_N_m=mg/h,source166_saved_Kforce_xx_N_m=2195.4086751955474,
   old166_value_source='Existing V3 audit166_142 saved source_model_audit output; original166 report NOT reread',derivatives=derivatives))
 R['own_authored_wheel_phase_counterexamples']=wheels
 # Literal necessary subset of the just-authorized once-read derived167
 # report. No original decision trajectory, q histories or measured effort.
 e=np.array([-.04837577206658796,-.910295600784066,.1125389728440375,-.3606965168503307,.11773020589499783,-.3404623354670532,
  .006738951348299094,-12.163273271144988,2.157783383890891,-4.786855912176916,2.0696344170371526,-5.060614494321986])
 contributions=-np.asarray(P['gain_tau6_by_state12'])*e[None,:];tau=np.asarray(P['nominal_static_tau6'])+contributions.sum(axis=1)
 R['last_saved167_error_gain_decomposition']=dict(derived_report_sha256='2799b7371ee99b9c4651047d5a3769b9c14d634f028fd7b3de3cf3df82650901',
  subset_source='Exact last_state_error12 in single allowed V4 derived167 read; copied literal for source arithmetic, not re-read',
  last_state_error12=e.tolist(),per_state_feedback_tau6_by12=contributions.tolist(),reconstructed_nominal_requested_tau6=tau.tolist(),
  actual_last_requested_torque_not_available_for_comparison=True,measured_actual_torque_unknown=True)
 R['conclusion']='Frozen material support-centre creates a wrong smooth positive gravity stiffness in the circle comparison; authored facet contact switching is nonsmooth and differs too. function [points,Jcontact,receipt]=own_phase_invariant_rolling_contact_tangent_v4(C,G,names,d)
% Ideal circular rolling PLANNING approximation, not original mesh contact.
% Recompute the current support point from the wheel axis/own radial shape.
% Do not differentiate a rotating fixed support-face average as a ground point.
names=cellstr(string(names));bn=cellstr(string(G.body_names));
assert(numel(names)==30&&numel(bn)==31);
shapes=G.shapes;if isstruct(shapes),shapes=num2cell(shapes);end
points=zeros(3,2);Jcontact=zeros(6,36);rows=cell(2,1);sides={'left','right'};
for j=1:2
 side=sides{j};wheel=[side,'_wheel'];joint=C.joints.([side,'_wheel_spin']);
 b=find(strcmp(bn,wheel),1);ids=find(cellfun(@(s)strcmp(s.body,wheel),shapes));assert(numel(ids)==1);
 shape=shapes{ids};R=d.body_rotation(:,:,b);p=d.body_position(:,b);
 localAxis=qm(joint.local_rot1)*[1;0;0];localAxis=localAxis/norm(localAxis);
 localCentre=joint.local_pos1(:);
 vertices=shape.linear_body_from_shape*shape.points.'+shape.origin_body_m(:);
 radial=vertices-localCentre;radial=radial-localAxis*(localAxis.'*radial);
 radius=max(vecnorm(radial));assert(radius>0&&isfinite(radius));
 axis=R*localAxis;down=[0;0;-1];radialDown=down-axis*(axis.'*down);assert(norm(radialDown)>1e-8);
 radialDown=radialDown/norm(radialDown);centre=p+R*localCentre;
 points(:,j)=centre+radius*radialDown;offset=points(:,j)-p;
 % The instantaneous contacting material velocity is zero. The support
 % location migrates as phase changes; its geometric time derivative is
 % not substituted as a material-point velocity in this Pfaffian row.
 Jcontact((j-1)*3+(1:3),:)=d.body_origin_Jv(:,:,b)-skew(offset)*d.body_Jw(:,:,b);
 rows{j}=struct('side',side,'radius_from_authored_radial_vertices_m',radius, ...
  'axis_world',axis,'centre_world',centre,'support_world',points(:,j), ...
  'support_phase','Recomputed current ideal circular support, not fixed wheel material point');
end
receipt=struct('model','OWN_GEOMETRY_IDEAL_CIRCULAR_ROLLING_PLANNING_APPROXIMATION_NOT_PHYSX', ...
 'wheels',{rows},'original_mesh_facet_and_contact_solver_not_reproduced',true, ...
 'actual_joint_limits_PD_gas_physics_not_modified',true,'physical_stability_proven',false);
end
function S=skew(v),v=v(:);S=[0,-v(3),v(2);v(3),0,-v(1);-v(2),v(1),0];end
function R=qm(q)
q=q(:)/norm(q);w=q(1);x=q(2);y=q(3);z=q(4);
R=[1-2*(y*y+z*z),2*(x*y-w*z),2*(x*z+w*y); ...
 2*(x*y+w*z),1-2*(x*x+z*z),2*(y*z-w*x); ...
 2*(x*z-w*y),2*(y*z+w*x),1-2*(x*x+y*y)];
end

# Pure six-action ground foundation for ROOT167

`source/own_mod4_lqr166_ground_controller_v3.py` exports `build_controller(parameters) -> controller.step(observation)`. Use `contracts/OWN_LQR166_INLINE_PARAMETERS_V3.json` and the exact cached-input contract. The module uses Python math only and performs no file, model, SDK, random or device operation. ROOT owns the original PhysX caller, GPU/resource admission and all Git archival. This package is locally frozen and **Git pending**, not a real physical balance or teacher success.

The six-action gain, static torques and leg coordinates come from ROOT166's actual own-model MATLAB computation. Four motor coordinates remain free in this model. State units are metres/radians and per-second rates; requested input is Nm; original raw action scaling is .5 rad and 10 rad/s. Target-space CAD limits and signed motor envelopes are retained. The nominal static projected residual is at most 1.31e-7 in mixed equation units. Virtual coordinates use the own hip and wheel joint anchors and match ROOT166 at the nominal pose within 2.78e-17.

The actual saved reset q4 is [-.2237882763,.3417617381,-.2237882763,.3417617381]. It is held for the contact-unknown bootstrap instead of being pulled to the old high stand. The first already-derived original callback has pitch .00530705554, roll .00139590399 and yaw .18000133336; its pitch is .17101987010 below the static .17632692565 target. That callback's q30 was not saved in the allowed summary, so it is not combined with reset q as a simultaneous measured state. Online control uses original physical RPY plus newly attached cached q/dq; the historical root13 quaternion label is ignored.

The independent LOCAL CPU purpose read the166 math report,142 derived report and own C/G small source JSON once each, after an exact local Python process receipt. It made no MATLAB, SDK, model, raw, remote or GPU call. Its cold counterexample explicitly combines exact initial q/dq/xyz with constructed pitch=0, rather than claiming an actual167 state. The first requested wheel torques are -6.313912/-6.315611 Nm and are clipped before inverse PD to the original -5.775910 Nm bound. `raw_saturation6=false` does not mean no effort clipping. The report records that initial envelope clipping; it does not count all pre-target clip controls. Its zero post-PD clip count is scoped to already pre-bounded targets.

The400-control/1600-callback nominal linear plant uses a two-callback **leg target queue**, zero wheel-target delay and current-state PD at each2.5ms substep, with both signed clips. Maximum nominal post-PD legs are [9.10925,3.34667,9.07697,3.33209] Nm and wheels both5.775910 Nm; final error is about2e-14. This is not a physical cold-reset PASS: the frozen material-contact linearization assumes no slip, fixed guide coordinates, local contact geometry and nominal delay/scales. It does not test wheel friction or nonlinear nonwheel collision. ROOT166's older whole-torque-delay spectral radius is retained as a differently scoped diagnostic. Same target offset .1 rad gives6 Nm at creation but2.8 Nm after q advances .02 rad and dq becomes1 rad/s; queuing creation torque would be a different system.

Named-order permutation, nonfinite/name/clock protection, authoritative original done, the genuine-air refusal, and float32-sized q-domain diagnostic behavior were checked in the one new CPU purpose. Actual q is never clamped and q-domain drift adds no physical termination. Unexpected real air requests a diagnostic stop because this component has no flight controller. Readonly position anchoring uses the first cached root state; no physical state is written.

The final backup goal remains acceleration, crouch/push, genuine double-wheel flight across270mm, landing and original top stability. V3 implements only the ground foundation. The future-stage contract keeps that distinction explicit; it does not hold up the separate MAIN165 SuperMimic training. ROOT167 original-plant evidence must determine slip, contact and cold-transition behavior before this is described as a physical backup.

def zoh(A,B,dt):
 # Local nominal linear plant only. Targets, not torques, are queued below.
 n,m=B.shape;Z=np.zeros((n+m,n+m));Z[:n,:n]=A;Z[:n,n:]=B;Z*=dt
 scale=max(0,int(math.ceil(math.log2(max(1.,float(np.linalg.norm(Z,np.inf)))))));Z/=2**scale
 E=np.eye(n+m);term=E.copy()
 for k in range(1,81):
  term=term@Z/k;E+=term
  if np.linalg.norm(term,np.inf)<1e-16:break
 require(k<80,'BoundedlocalZOHseriesconvergence')
 for _ in range(scale):E=E@E
 return E[:n,:n],E[:n,n:]

"""Pure six-action ground FOUNDATION; no SDK/torch/model/file/RNG operation.
Pose comes fromoriginalphysicalcallbackRPY, never historicalquat-labelguess.
Own30jointimportT0/T1 anchors definelegs, not AABBcentres orforeignfivebar.
"""
import math

ACTION_ORDER=('left_crank','left_hip','right_crank','right_hip','left_wheel_spin','right_wheel_spin')
SCHEMA='mod4.own_lqr166.ground_foundation_parameters.v3'
def require(v,m):
    if not v:raise ValueError(m)
def finite(v,n,label):
    require(isinstance(v,(list,tuple)) and len(v)==n and all(isinstance(x,(int,float)) and not isinstance(x,bool) and math.isfinite(x) for x in v),label)
    return tuple(float(x) for x in v)
def add(a,b):return tuple(x+y for x,y in zip(a,b))
def sub(a,b):return tuple(x-y for x,y in zip(a,b))
def mul(a,s):return tuple(x*s for x in a)
def dot(a,b):return sum(x*y for x,y in zip(a,b))
def cross(a,b):return (a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0])
def mv(R,v):return tuple(dot(row,v) for row in R)
def transpose(R):return tuple(zip(*R))
def mm(A,B):return tuple(tuple(dot(row,col) for col in zip(*B)) for row in A)
def qmat(q):
    n=math.sqrt(dot(q,q));require(n>0,'Ownsourcejointquaternion');w,x,y,z=(v/n for v in q)
    return ((1-2*(y*y+z*z),2*(x*y-w*z),2*(x*z+w*y)),(2*(x*y+w*z),1-2*(x*x+z*z),2*(y*z-w*x)),(2*(x*z-w*y),2*(y*z+w*x),1-2*(x*x+y*y)))
def rpy_matrix(roll,pitch,yaw):
    cr,sr,cp,sp,cy,sy=math.cos(roll),math.sin(roll),math.cos(pitch),math.sin(pitch),math.cos(yaw),math.sin(yaw)
    return ((cy*cp,cy*sp*sr-sy*cr,cy*sp*cr+sy*sr),(sy*cp,sy*sp*sr+cy*cr,sy*sp*cr-cy*sr),(-sp,cp*sr,cp*cr))
def angle_error(a,b):return math.atan2(math.sin(a-b),math.cos(a-b))
def clip(x,lo,hi):return max(lo,min(hi,x))

class OwnLQR166GroundFoundation:
    def __init__(self,parameters):
        require(parameters['schema']==SCHEMA,'Typed166parametersrequired');self.p=parameters
        require(parameters['source_mjcf_sha256']=='fbb0393883d31ac96f8cd58a184585470222f7daf96af6aa5d0748a1752c366c','OwnMOD4identity')
        self.names=tuple(parameters['joint_names']);self.bodies=tuple(parameters['body_names']);self.joints=parameters['fk_joints']
        require(len(self.names)==30 and len(set(self.names))==30 and set(self.names)==set(self.joints),'Namedphysical30')
        require(len(self.bodies)==31 and self.bodies[0]=='chassis','Own31bodytree')
        self.ids=tuple(self.names.index(n) for n in ACTION_ORDER);self.K=tuple(finite(r,12,'gainrow') for r in parameters['gain_tau6_by_state12']);require(len(self.K)==6,'gain6x12')
        self.tau0=finite(parameters['nominal_static_tau6'],6,'tau0');self.reference=finite(parameters['reference_virtual_y6'],6,'virtualtarget')
        self.effort=finite(parameters['effort_limit6'],6,'effort6');self.stall=finite(parameters['saturation_effort6'],6,'stall6');self.speed=finite(parameters['no_load_rad_s6'],6,'speed6')
        require(all(x>0 for x in (*self.effort,*self.stall,*self.speed)),'Positiveoriginalmotorparameters')
        require(parameters['control_dt_s']==.01 and parameters['diagnostic_controls']==400,'Explicitground400diagnostic')
        require(parameters['selected_ground_velocity_m_s']==0 and parameters['historical_public_command_m_s']==.5,'Explicitdiagnosticgoalnotjumpcommand')
        self.rows=[];bychild={r['child']:(n,r) for n,r in self.joints.items()};seen={'chassis'}
        for child in self.bodies[1:]:
            n,r=bychild[child];require(r['parent'] in seen,'Owndatatopologicalorder');seen.add(child)
            self.rows.append((n,r,self.bodies.index(r['parent']),qmat(r['local_rot0']),transpose(qmat(r['local_rot1']))))
        self.last_control=None;self.episode=None;self.anchor=None;self.anchor_yaw=None

    def _named(self,names,q,dq=None):
        names=tuple(names);q=finite(q,30,'finiteactualq30');require(len(names)==30 and len(set(names))==30 and set(names)==set(self.names),'Exactactualjointnames')
        perm=[names.index(n) for n in self.names];q=tuple(q[i] for i in perm)
        if dq is not None:dq=finite(dq,30,'finiteactualdq30');dq=tuple(dq[i] for i in perm)
        return q,dq

    def _fk(self,p,R,v,w,q,dq):
        P=[p];Rs=[R];V=[v];W=[w]
        for n,r,parent,R0,R1T in self.rows:
            j=self.names.index(n);a=q[j];ad=dq[j];A=mm(Rs[parent],R0);axis=tuple(row[0] for row in A);anchor=mv(Rs[parent],r['local_pos0'])
            if r['type']=='hinge':
                c,s=math.cos(a),math.sin(a);CR=mm(mm(A,((1.,0.,0.),(0.,c,-s),(0.,s,c))),R1T);cw=add(W[parent],mul(axis,ad));offset=mv(CR,r['local_pos1']);cp=sub(add(P[parent],anchor),offset)
                cv=sub(add(V[parent],cross(W[parent],anchor)),cross(cw,offset))
            elif r['type']=='slide':
                CR=mm(A,R1T);cw=W[parent];offset=sub(add(anchor,mul(axis,a)),mv(CR,r['local_pos1']));cp=add(P[parent],offset);cv=add(add(V[parent],cross(W[parent],offset)),mul(axis,ad))
            else:raise ValueError('Ownhinge/slideonly')
            P.append(cp);Rs.append(CR);V.append(cv);W.append(cw)
        return P,Rs,V,W

    def _virtual(self,p,R,v,w,q,dq):
        P,Rs,V,W=self._fk(p,R,v,w,q,dq);bx=tuple(row[0] for row in R);h=math.hypot(bx[0],bx[1]);require(h>1e-8,'Headingundefined')
        ex=(bx[0]/h,bx[1]/h,0.);ey=(-ex[1],ex[0],0.);bxd=cross(w,bx);yawdot=(bx[0]*bxd[1]-bx[1]*bxd[0])/(h*h);exd=mul(ey,yawdot)
        lengths=[];theta=[];ld=[];td=[]
        def anchor(joint):
            row=self.joints[joint];b=self.bodies.index(row['parent']);offset=mv(Rs[b],row['local_pos0']);return add(P[b],offset),add(V[b],cross(W[b],offset))
        for side in ('left','right'):
            hp,hv=anchor(side+'_hip');ap,av=anchor(side+'_wheel_spin');r=sub(hp,ap);rd=sub(hv,av);L=math.sqrt(dot(r,r));rx=dot(r,ex);rz=r[2];require(L>.01 and rx*rx+rz*rz>1e-12,'Nondegenerateownleg')
            lengths.append(L);theta.append(math.atan2(rx,rz));ld.append(dot(r,rd)/L);drx=dot(rd,ex)+dot(r,exd);td.append((rz*drx-rx*rd[2])/(rx*rx+rz*rz))
        return lengths,theta,ld,td,dot(w,ey)

    def _signed_clip(self,tau,velocity):
        out=[]
        for t,v,e,s,no in zip(tau,velocity,self.effort,self.stall,self.speed):
            vv=clip(v,-no*(1+e/s),no*(1+e/s));hi=min(s*(1-vv/no),e);lo=max(s*(-1-vv/no),-e);out.append(clip(t,lo,hi))
        return tuple(out)

    def _raw_from_torque(self,q,dq,requested):
        velocity=tuple(dq[i] for i in self.ids);bounded=self._signed_clip(requested,velocity)
        target=tuple(q[self.ids[j]]+(bounded[j]+2*dq[self.ids[j]])/60 for j in range(4))
        wheels=tuple(velocity[4+j]+bounded[4+j]/.6 for j in range(2));raw_before=tuple(x/.5 for x in target)+tuple(x/10 for x in wheels)
        raw=tuple(clip(x,-6,6) for x in raw_before);processed=list(tuple(.5*x for x in raw[:4])+tuple(10*x for x in raw[4:]))
        for j in (0,2):processed[j]=clip(processed[j],processed[j+1]-1.0843,processed[j+1]+.85)
        # Send the already-CAD-projected targets throughoriginalrawcodec.
        final=tuple(processed[j]/(.5 if j<4 else 10.) for j in range(6))
        nominal=tuple(60*(processed[j]-q[self.ids[j]])-2*dq[self.ids[j]] for j in range(4))+tuple(.6*(processed[j]-velocity[j]) for j in (4,5))
        nominal=self._signed_clip(self._signed_clip(nominal,velocity),velocity)
        return final,dict(requested_nominal_torque6=list(requested),bounded_nominal_torque6=list(bounded),nominal_after_PD_CAD_two_clips6=list(nominal),
            raw_before_limits6=list(raw_before),raw_saturation6=[abs(a-b)>1e-10 for a,b in zip(raw_before,raw)],
            nominal_torque_limit_active6=[abs(a-b)>1e-10 for a,b in zip(requested,bounded)],
            CAD_projection_active2=[abs(processed[j]-.5*raw[j])>1e-10 for j in (0,2)],
            processed_target6=processed,nominal_not_measured_effort=True)

    def step(self,observation):
        kind=observation['kind']
        if kind=='initial_contact_unknown':
            require(self.last_control is None,'Bootstraponlyonce');q,_=self._named(observation['joint_names'],observation['q30']);self.last_control=0
            raw=tuple(clip(q[i]/.5,-6,6) for i in self.ids[:4])+(0.,0.)
            return dict(raw6=list(raw),phase='BOOTSTRAP_ACTUAL_Q_HOLD_CONTACT_UNKNOWN',global_control=0,successful_skill=False,ground_foundation_only=True)
        require(kind=='original_cached_physical','Originalcachedcallbackonly');o=observation['source_observation']
        control=o['global_control'];require(type(control) is int and self.last_control is not None and control==self.last_control+1,'Exactnext-actionclock')
        done=o.get('original_done',o.get('actual_done'));require(type(done) is bool and not done,'Noactionafteractualoriginaldone')
        episode=o['episode_id'];require(type(episode) is int,'Actualepisodeid');require(self.episode is None or episode==self.episode,'Actualepisodechanged, ROOTmustbootstrapnewcase')
        names=o.get('named_joint_order',o.get('joint_names'));q,dq=self._named(names,o['q30'],o['dq30'])
        p=finite(o['actual_root_xyz'],3,'Actualcachedrootxyz');v=finite(o['world_velocity3'],3,'Actualcachedworldv');w=finite(o['world_angular_velocity3'],3,'Actualcachedworldomega')
        roll,pitch,yaw=(float(o[k]) for k in ('root_roll','root_pitch','root_yaw'));require(all(math.isfinite(x) for x in (roll,pitch,yaw)),'PhysicalRPYfinite')
        air=o['clean_air_now'];require(type(air) is bool,'Originalairboolnotfakezero')
        if self.anchor is None:self.anchor=p;self.anchor_yaw=yaw;self.episode=episode
        self.last_control=control
        if air:
            raw=tuple(clip(q[i]/.5,-6,6) for i in self.ids[:4])+(clip(dq[self.ids[4]]/10,-6,6),clip(dq[self.ids[5]]/10,-6,6))
            return dict(raw6=list(raw),phase='ACTUAL_AIR_OUTSIDE_GROUND_FOUNDATION',diagnostic_stop_requested=True,
                stop_reason='Genuineairneedsseparatestagedbackupcontroller; notfixedgaingroundmodel',successful_skill=False,ground_foundation_only=True)
        R=rpy_matrix(roll,pitch,yaw);L,theta,Ld,thetad,phid=self._virtual(p,R,v,w,q,dq);ex=(math.cos(self.anchor_yaw),math.sin(self.anchor_yaw),0.)
        actual=(dot(sub(p,self.anchor),ex),pitch,L[0],theta[0],L[1],theta[1]);rates=(dot(v,ex),phid,Ld[0],thetad[0],Ld[1],thetad[1])
        error=list(a-b for a,b in zip(actual,self.reference));error[1]=angle_error(actual[1],self.reference[1]);error[3]=angle_error(actual[3],self.reference[3]);error[5]=angle_error(actual[5],self.reference[5]);error=tuple(error)+tuple(rates)
        requested=tuple(t-dot(k,error) for t,k in zip(self.tau0,self.K));raw,details=self._raw_from_torque(q,dq,requested)
        require(all(math.isfinite(x) and abs(x)<=6.0000001 for x in raw),'Originalfiniteaction6bounds')
        return dict(raw6=list(raw),phase='OWN_COUPLED_LQR_ZERO_GROUND_HOLD_FOUNDATION',global_control=control,episode_id=episode,
            state_error12=list(error),measured_virtual_y6=list(actual),measured_virtual_velocity6=list(rates),target_virtual_y6=list(self.reference),
            readonly_initial_position_anchor3=list(self.anchor),actual_root_rpy_source='originalphysicalcallback, notroot13quatlabel',
            raw_saturation6=details['raw_saturation6'],requested_nominal_torque6=details['requested_nominal_torque6'],nominal_details=details,
            actual_air=air,actual_prepared=o.get('prepared_now'),actual_public_request=o.get('request_active'),
            selected_diagnostic_goal='zero-groundhold; originalpublic0p5commandunchanged',yaw_full_feedback_not_claimed=True,
            actual_qdomain_diagnostic_only=[self.names[j] for j,n in enumerate(self.names) if self.joints[n].get('limits_si') and not self.joints[n]['limits_si'][0]<=q[j]<=self.joints[n]['limits_si'][1]],
            qdomain_diagnostic_not_added_physical_termination=True,
            ground_foundation_only=True,successful_skill=False,full270backup_requires_separate_accel_push_air_landing_validation=True)


def build_controller(parameters):
    return OwnLQR166GroundFoundation(parameters)

===== FULL UTF8 C:\Users\lulu\Desktop\claude\mjcf\MOD4_H1_SCUT_STEP270_ON_210_E3_CONTINUATION_20261021\OWN_MOD4_VIRTUAL_LEG_LQR_BACKUP_SOURCE_V2_20261030\source\own_mod4_virtual_leg_coordinates_v2.py SEGMENT 0 =====
"""Own hip-anchor to wheel-axle virtualcoordinates, noforeign geometry.
Consumes ownFK positions/velocities already computed from existingstate.
No SDK/contact query, model, controllerforce, RNG or plantstate operation.
"""
import torch


class OwnVirtualLegCoordinates:
    def __init__(self,contract,body_names):
        if contract['source_mjcf_sha256']!='fbb0393883d31ac96f8cd58a184585470222f7daf96af6aa5d0748a1752c366c':
            raise ValueError('Exact ownMOD4 source required')
        self.C=contract;self.body_names=tuple(body_names)
        if len(self.body_names)!=31:raise ValueError('Own31body order')

    def _anchor(self,g,joint):
        row=self.C['joints'][joint];b=self.body_names.index(row['parent'])
        R=g['body_rotation'][...,b,:,:];offset=(R@R.new_tensor(row['local_pos0'])[...,None]).squeeze(-1)
        p=g['body_position'][...,b,:]+offset
        v=g['body_linear_velocity'][...,b,:]+torch.cross(g['body_angular_velocity'][...,b,:],offset,dim=-1)
        return p,v

    def measure(self,g):
        R=g['body_rotation'][...,0,:,:];w=g['root_angular_velocity'];x=R[...,0]
        flat=x.clone();flat[...,2]=0
        horizontal=flat.norm(dim=-1,keepdim=True)
        if not bool((horizontal>1e-8).all()):raise ValueError('Heading undefined atverticalbodyx')
        ex=flat/horizontal;ey=torch.stack((-ex[...,1],ex[...,0],torch.zeros_like(ex[...,0])),-1)
        xd=torch.cross(w,x,dim=-1)
        yawdot=(x[...,0]*xd[...,1]-x[...,1]*xd[...,0])/horizontal[...,0].square()
        exdot=ey*yawdot[...,None]
        lengths=[];theta=[];beta=[];ld=[];td=[];bd=[];height=[];hd=[];lateral=[];hips=[];axles=[]
        for side in ('left','right'):
            hp,hv=self._anchor(g,side+'_hip');ap,av=self._anchor(g,side+'_wheel_spin')
            r=hp-ap;rv=hv-av;local=(R.transpose(-1,-2)@r[...,None]).squeeze(-1)
            localv=(R.transpose(-1,-2)@(rv-torch.cross(w,r,dim=-1))[...,None]).squeeze(-1)
            L=r.norm(dim=-1);rx=(r*ex).sum(-1);rz=r[...,2];drx=(rv*ex).sum(-1)+(r*exdot).sum(-1)
            if not bool((L>.01).all()):raise ValueError('Degenerateownleglength')
            lengths.append(L);theta.append(torch.atan2(rx,rz));beta.append(torch.atan2(local[...,0],local[...,2]))
            ld.append((r*rv).sum(-1)/L)
            td.append((rz*drx-rx*rv[...,2])/(rx.square()+rz.square()).clamp_min(1e-12))
            bd.append((local[...,2]*localv[...,0]-local[...,0]*localv[...,2])/(local[...,0].square()+local[...,2].square()).clamp_min(1e-12))
            height.append(rz);hd.append(rv[...,2]);lateral.append(local[...,1]);hips.append(hp);axles.append(ap)
        return dict(length2=torch.stack(lengths,-1),theta_world2=torch.stack(theta,-1),beta_body2=torch.stack(beta,-1),
            length_rate2=torch.stack(ld,-1),theta_world_rate2=torch.stack(td,-1),beta_body_rate2=torch.stack(bd,-1),
            hip_height_above_axle2=torch.stack(height,-1),hip_height_rate2=torch.stack(hd,-1),
            body_lateral_offset2=torch.stack(lateral,-1),hip_world2=torch.stack(hips,-2),axle_world2=torch.stack(axles,-2),
            heading_yaw_rate=yawdot,body_pitch_rate=(w*ey).sum(-1),heading_x=ex,heading_y=ey,
            actual_contact_or_support=False)

% Independent BACKUP calculation only. Never a SuperMimic learning gate.
% Fourlegmotor coordinates remainfree; noforeign mass/gain/JumpF orplantwrite.

 [P,m]=readJSON(fullfile(T,'H1_MATLAB_ACTUAL_INITIAL_BALANCE_EXECUTION_V143_20261030','ACTUAL_INITIAL_STATIC_SOURCE_V4A_ACTUAL.json'),'7457f690494bb5892ad1ef8c217b2d10dc97435123b5cc9383b09e3bc7f36375','own143static_math');inputs(end+1)=m;
 [C,m]=readJSON(fullfile(B,'job0','assets','import_contract.json'),'517cd6a4cc469455af08da75dc4b7f7cb596b375bafb4c3e566c7df5bc51df49','own_C');inputs(end+1)=m;
 [G,m]=readJSON(fullfile(B,'source','OWN_STEP_WHEEL_GEOMETRY_FROM_R2_SOURCE.json'),'461f6b44e6014c72a755a0db5136fa8d45bd5b53b6318ee4b38221a4c597bc4d','own_G');inputs(end+1)=m;
 [~,m]=readBytes(matrixFile,'0cd6eb40378130064a5e327c04508bf0682901ad5b51c8d298e72d7d0a238ef4','own_gas2000_M_h_J');inputs(end+1)=m;
 [~,m]=readBytes(clipFile,'257a9753b8264d65634f01edf8ce47e4a27e3ee3a1f81bfcace42a090770015f','captured210signedclip_not72APIequivalence');inputs(end+1)=m;

 assert(~P.static_solution.used_soft_residual_fallback&&P.static_solution.hard_exitflag>0);
 names=cellstr(string(P.named_joint_order));order={'left_crank','left_hip','right_crank','right_hip','left_wheel_spin','right_wheel_spin'};
 [found,act]=ismember(order,names);assert(all(found));guide=find(startsWith(names,'guide_roller_'));dep=setdiff(1:30,[act(:);guide(:)]);assert(numel(dep)==16&&numel(guide)==8);
 state=struct('p',P.reference_root_position_OFFLINE_ONLY(:),'R',P.reference_R_world_from_chassis,'v',zeros(3,1),'w',zeros(3,1),'q',P.actual_q30(:),'dq',zeros(30,1));

function m=groundModel(context,s)
C=context.C;G=context.G;names=context.names;d=own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1(C,G,names,s);[pc,JC]=contactJ(G,d,context.pointLocal);
guide=context.guide;guideRows=zeros(8,36);for j=1:8,guideRows(j,6+guide(j))=1;end
v=virtualJ(C,G,d);Aall=[d.loop_J(context.loopRows,:);JC;guideRows;v.yawRow];
if isempty(context.rowsA)
 [~,rr,pivot]=qr(Aall.','vector');dd=abs(diag(rr));tolerance=1e-8*max([dd;1]);rankA=sum(dd>tolerance);rowsA=pivot(1:rankA);
else,rowsA=context.rowsA;rankA=numel(rowsA);tolerance=[];end
As=Aall(rowsA,:);[~,~,V]=svd(As);N=V(:,rankA+1:end);assert(size(N,2)==6,'Groundreduction6DOFchanged; preservefalse, nevermodifyplant.');
O=zeros(6,36);O(1,1:3)=v.headingX.';O(2,4:6)=v.headingY.';
for j=1:4,O(2+j,6+context.act(j))=1;end
T=N/(O*N);transform=[O(1:2,:)*T;v.Jworld*T];Tv=T/transform;
m=struct('coordinate_order_motor',{{'root_forward_x','body_phi','left_crank','left_hip','right_crank','right_hip'}}, ...
 'coordinate_order_virtual',{{'root_forward_x','body_phi','left_L','left_theta_world','right_L','right_theta_world'}}, ...
 'null_dimension',size(N,2),'constraint_rank',rankA,'independent_constraint_rows',rowsA,'nominal_rank_tolerance',tolerance, ...
 'Tmotor',T,'Tvirtual',Tv,'Mvirtual',Tv.'*d.M*Tv,'Bvirtual',Tv.'*d.B_action_torque_order6, ...
 'Mvirtual_eigenvalues',eig(Tv.'*d.M*Tv),'input_projection_rank',rank(Tv.'*d.B_action_torque_order6), ...
 'gravity_passive_h_virtual',Tv.'*d.h,'gas_virtual',Tv.'*d.gas_generalized_force,'virtual',v, ...
 'max_selected_constraint_tangent',max(abs(As*Tv),[],'all'),'max_full31_constraint_tangent',max(abs(Aall*Tv),[],'all'), ...
 'max_all22_loop_tangent',max(abs(d.loop_J*Tv),[],'all'),'contactWorld',pc, ...
 'all6_anchor_m',vecnorm(reshape(d.loop_phi(1:18),3,6)),'all_mimic',d.loop_phi(19:end), ...
 'fourlegmotors_locked',false,'guide_rates_locked_planningonly',true,'yaw_rate_locked_planningonly',true);
end
function [f,receipt]=projectedForce(context,s,tau)
checkQ(context.C,context.names,s.q,context.act);m=groundModel(context,s);d=own_mod4_tree_dynamics_v1(context.C,context.G,context.names,s);
f=m.Tvirtual.'*(d.gas_generalized_force-d.h+d.B_action_torque_order6*tau);
receipt=struct('all6_anchor_m',m.all6_anchor_m,'all_mimic',m.all_mimic,'full_constraint_tangent',m.max_full31_constraint_tangent,'q_domain_valid',true,'no_actual_plant_query',true);
end
function s=perturbState(s,dx)
s.p=s.p+dx(1:3);s.R=expm(skew(dx(4:6)))*s.R;s.q=s.q+dx(7:36);
end
function v=virtualJ(C,G,d)
names=cellstr(string(G.body_names));R=d.body_rotation(:,:,1);x=R(:,1);horizontal=hypot(x(1),x(2));assert(horizontal>1e-8);
ex=[x(1);x(2);0]/horizontal;ey=[-ex(2);ex(1);0];ez=[0;0;1];yawRow=zeros(1,36);gyaw=[-x(2);x(1);0]/horizontal^2;yawRow(4:6)=cross(x,gyaw).';
JrootW=zeros(3,36);JrootW(:,4:6)=eye(3);Jworld=zeros(4,36);Jbody=Jworld;Jheight=zeros(2,36);axle=zeros(3,2);Jaxle=zeros(3,36,2);
L=zeros(2,1);theta=L;beta=L;h=L;lateral=L;hip=zeros(3,2);
for j=1:2
 sides={'left','right'};side=sides{j};[hp,Jh]=anchor(C.joints.([side,'_hip']));[ap,Ja]=anchor(C.joints.([side,'_wheel_spin']));
 r=hp-ap;Jr=Jh-Ja;rho=R.'*r;Jrho=R.'*(Jr+skew(r)*JrootW);rx=ex.'*r;rz=r(3);ry=ey.'*r;
 L(j)=norm(r);theta(j)=atan2(rx,rz);beta(j)=atan2(rho(1),rho(3));h(j)=rz;lateral(j)=rho(2);
 Jworld(2*j-1,:)=r.'/L(j)*Jr;Jbody(2*j-1,:)=Jworld(2*j-1,:);
 Jworld(2*j,:)=((rz*ex.'-rx*ez.')*Jr+rz*ry*yawRow)/(rx^2+rz^2);
 Jbody(2*j,:)=(rho(3)*[1,0,0]-rho(1)*[0,0,1])*Jrho/(rho(1)^2+rho(3)^2);
 Jheight(j,:)=ez.'*Jr;hip(:,j)=hp;axle(:,j)=ap;Jaxle(:,:,j)=Ja;
end
v=struct('length2',L,'theta_world2',theta,'beta_body2',beta,'hip_height_above_axle2',h,'body_lateral_offset2',lateral, ...
 'body_phi',atan2(-R(3,1),horizontal),'headingX',ex,'headingY',ey,'yawRow',yawRow,'Jworld',Jworld,'Jbody',Jbody, ...
 'JhipHeight',Jheight,'hipWorld',hip,'axleWorld',axle,'Jaxle',Jaxle);
 function [p,J]=anchor(row)
  b=find(strcmp(names,row.parent),1);offset=d.body_rotation(:,:,b)*row.local_pos0(:);p=d.body_position(:,b)+offset;J=d.body_origin_Jv(:,:,b)-skew(offset)*d.body_Jw(:,:,b);
 end
end
function local=nominalContactLocal(G,d)
shapes=G.shapes;if isstruct(shapes),shapes=num2cell(shapes);end
names=cellstr(string(G.body_names));local=zeros(3,2);wheels={'left_wheel','right_wheel'};
for j=1:2
 b=find(strcmp(names,wheels{j}),1);ids=find(cellfun(@(s)strcmp(s.body,wheels{j}),shapes));assert(numel(ids)==1);s=shapes{ids};R=d.body_rotation(:,:,b);p=d.body_position(:,b);
 vertices=R*s.linear_body_from_shape*s.points.'+p+R*s.origin_body_m(:);point=mean(vertices(:,abs(vertices(3,:)-min(vertices(3,:)))<1e-12),2);local(:,j)=R.'*(point-p);
end
end
function [points,JC]=contactJ(G,d,local)
names=cellstr(string(G.body_names));wheels={'left_wheel','right_wheel'};points=zeros(3,2);JC=zeros(6,36);
for j=1:2,b=find(strcmp(names,wheels{j}),1);offset=d.body_rotation(:,:,b)*local(:,j);points(:,j)=d.body_position(:,b)+offset;JC((j-1)*3+(1:3),:)=d.body_origin_Jv(:,:,b)-skew(offset)*d.body_Jw(:,:,b);end
end
function result=linearClipDiagnostic(C,names,s,act,Tv,tau0,Ad,Bd,K)
state=zeros(12,1);state([2,4,6])=-.03;previous=zeros(6,1);peak=[C.leg_motor.peak_torque_nm*ones(4,1);C.wheel_motor.peak_torque_nm*ones(2,1)];
stall=[C.leg_motor.envelope_intercept_nm*ones(4,1);C.wheel_motor.envelope_intercept_nm*ones(2,1)];speed=[C.leg_motor.no_load_speed_rpm*ones(4,1);C.wheel_motor.no_load_speed_rpm*ones(2,1)]*2*pi/60;
maxTau=zeros(6,1);rawMax=zeros(6,1);maxError=zeros(12,1);domainViolations=0;clipTicks=0;
for tick=1:400
 q=s.q+Tv(7:36,:)*state(1:6);vel=Tv*state(7:12);dq=vel(7:36);requested=tau0-K*state;
 target=q(act(1:4))+(requested(1:4)+2*dq(act(1:4)))/60;wheel=dq(act(5:6))+requested(5:6)/.6;raw=[target/.5;wheel/10];rawMax=max(rawMax,abs(raw));raw=max(-6,min(6,raw));processed=[.5*raw(1:4);10*raw(5:6)];
 for j=[1,3],processed(j)=min(processed(j+1)+.85,max(processed(j+1)-1.0843,processed(j)));end
 effort=[60*(processed(1:4)-q(act(1:4)))-2*dq(act(1:4));.6*(processed(5:6)-dq(act(5:6)))];
 [first,~]=dc_motor_signed_clip_from210_v1(effort,dq(act),peak,stall,speed);[actual,~]=dc_motor_signed_clip_from210_v1(first,dq(act),peak,stall,speed);
 if max(abs(actual-requested))>1e-6,clipTicks=clipTicks+1;end
 if ~withinQ(C,names,q,act),domainViolations=domainViolations+1;end
 maxTau=max(maxTau,abs(actual));maxError=max(maxError,abs(state));state=Ad*state+Bd*previous;previous=actual-tau0;
 if any(~isfinite(state)),break;end
end
result=struct('steps_completed',tick,'dt_s',.01,'initial_body_pitch_and_both_worldleg_errors_rad',-.03,'max_abs_state12',maxError,'final_state12',state, ...
 'max_abs_actual_nominal_tau6',maxTau,'max_abs_requested_raw6',rawMax,'clip_ticks',clipTicks,'q_domain_violation_ticks',domainViolations, ...
 'scope','400stepclippedlinearbackupdiagnostic withonecontroldelay, notexactoriginal2callbacklegdelay/DR/nativeplant/physics', ...
 'physical_stability_proven',false,'primary_SM_gate',false);
end
function yes=withinQ(C,names,q,act)
yes=all(isfinite(q));for j=1:30,lim=C.joints.(names{j}).limits_si;if ~isempty(lim)&&~(q(j)>=lim(1)&&q(j)<=lim(2)),yes=false;end,end
a=q(act(1:4));yes=yes&&all(a>=[-1.2;-.5;-1.2;-.5]&a<=[.9;.5;.9;.5])&&all(a([1,3])-a([2,4])>=-1.0843&a([1,3])-a([2,4])<=.85);
end
function checkQ(C,names,q,act),assert(withinQ(C,names,q,act),'Originaljoint/CADdomain; no qclamp.');end
function S=skew(v),v=v(:);S=[0,-v(3),v(2);v(3),0,-v(1);-v(2),v(1),0];end
function [v,m]=readJSON(path,expected,key),[b,m]=readBytes(path,expected,key);v=jsondecode(native2unicode(b(:).','UTF-8'));end
function [b,m]=readBytes(path,expected,key)

end
function s=digest(b),md=java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance('SHA-256');md.update(typecast(uint8(b(:)),'int8'));s=lower(reshape(dec2hex(typecast(md.digest(),'uint8'),2).',1,[]));end
function yes=samePath(a,b),yes=strcmpi(strrep(a,'\','/'),strrep(b,'\','/'));end
function a=writeJSON(path,v)
assert(~isfile(path)&&~isfolder(path));f=fopen(path,'wb');assert(f>=0);cleanup=onCleanup(@()fclose(f)); %#ok<NASGU>
b=[unicode2native(jsonencode(v,'PrettyPrint',true),'UTF-8'),uint8(10)];assert(fwrite(f,b,'uint8')==numel(b));a=struct('path',path,'bytes',numel(b),'sha256',digest(b),'typed_kind','OWN_INDEPENDENT_BACKUP_MATH_NOT_ACTUALCONTROL_OR_SM_GATE');
end

def assign(s,key,value):

def read(p):return json.loads(Path(p).read_bytes())
def H(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def put(p,v):
    p=Path(p);p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    with p.open('xb') as f:f.write((json.dumps(v,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
def write(p,s):
    p=Path(p);p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);p.write_bytes(s.encode())
def rep(s,a,b):
    assert s.count(a)==1,(a,s.count(a));return s.replace(a,b)
def assign(s,key,value):
    node=next(x for x in ast.parse(s).body if isinstance(x,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(n,ast.Name) and n.id==key for n in x.targets))
    lines=s.splitlines(True)
    return ''.join(lines[:node.lineno-1])+key+'='+pprint.pformat(value,width=120,sort_dicts=False)+'\n'+''.join(lines[node.end_lineno:])
